##### Unstated assumptions

**PR-HIGH-051 — Interrupted replay leaves an independently decryptable recording after archive deletion**

- Plan section: D11; C5; Task 4.1a.
- Materiality: build-affecting
- Why it matters: Replay writes another audio copy under an independent, persisted DPAPI key. Terminating the process or losing power after that write bypasses Python teardown. The temporary directory survives, but neither Delete recording nor Delete now destroys its independent key. A recording can therefore remain recoverable after the practitioner deletes it following withdrawal. This is separate from the handled teardown failure already amended in round 1.
- Current plan text: `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:760`: “it creates AND destroys its own temporary store on every path”; `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:185`: “Deleting the entry key destroys the audio on every build”.
- Evidence:
  - `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/speaker_eval.py:1090`: `temp_root = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=TEMP_DIR_PREFIX))`
  - `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/speaker_eval.py:1094`: `wrap_key_to_file(crypto, session_dir)`
  - `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/speaker_eval.py:1095`: `_write_store(session_dir, crypto, session_id, pcm)`
  - `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/speaker_eval.py:1121–1122`:
    ```python
    except BaseException:
        destroy_temporary_store(temp_root, session_dir, crypto)
    ```
  - `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/speaker_eval.py:955`: `"its key blob is STILL PRESENT, so the store is recoverable by this Windows user"`
  - The existing documented interruption is specifically `docs/security/threat-model.md:3514`: “interrupted run (Ctrl+C) prints one fixed line”. It does not cover termination that prevents cleanup and diagnostics from executing.
- Suggested change: Give replay’s temporary audio a memory-only key, without an independently recoverable DPAPI blob, or explicitly bind its persisted key to the archive audio key and provide restart cleanup. Amend the helper contract and relevant guards together. Add a synthetic interrupted-replay test proving that surviving temporary files cannot remain decryptable after recording deletion; preserve ordinary teardown diagnostics.
- /fix decision: Apply (plan amendment, verified against the code by the owning planning session, 2026-10-07)
- /fix notes: Confirmed: the helper writes a DPAPI blob (`speaker_eval.py:1090-1095`) and `transcribe_session(session_dir, crypto, …)` takes the key in memory, so the blob serves only `SessionChunkStore.create`'s `require_key` guard. Task 4.1a, D11 and C5 now give the helper `persist_key=False` for the replay tool (no key file; `create(..., require_key=False)`, its docstring widened; `destroy_temporary_store` tolerant), so an interrupted run leaves nothing decryptable; `measure-speakers.py` keeps the default; the threat model's helper contract gains the two modes; an interrupted-run test is named.

##### Practicality / feasibility / sequencing

**PR-MED-061 — An unresolved export marker can disappear before recovery uses it**

- Plan section: D8, D10; Tasks 2.2 and 3.3.
- Materiality: build-affecting
- Why it matters: Task 3.3 explicitly permits failed immediate cleanup, leaving an app-owned plaintext `.part`. Its sole recovery pointer is inside the archive entry. Delete now or expiry removes that pointer with the entry, so the promised startup warning for a marker whose entry key was deleted cannot reliably occur. A subsequent export to another folder also needs an explicit rule preventing replacement of the unresolved marker. Otherwise the round-5 fix loses track of the first partial export.
- Current plan text: `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:818`:
  > BEFORE any plaintext is written, `store.begin_export(id, folder)` records the destination folder in the entry as `export.pending`

  The same line states:
  > a marker it cannot decrypt (the entry key gone to Delete now or expiry) is removed and ONE content-free start-up line says a partial export file may remain in a folder the practitioner chose

  D8, `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:151`:
  > `delete_entry` (Delete now) and the retention sweep are unchanged in the store and the UI
- Evidence: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:716–717`:
  ```python
  entry = self._committed(session_id)
  if not _remove_key_first(entry):
  ```
  `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:952`:
  ```python
  (directory / KEY_FILENAME).unlink(missing_ok=True)
  ```
  `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:957`:
  ```python
  shutil.rmtree(directory, ignore_errors=True)
  ```
  This deletes `export.pending` itself. Additionally, `reconcile_pending` removes keyless directories at `past_sessions.py:628`:
  ```python
  shutil.rmtree(entry, ignore_errors=True)
  ```
- Suggested change: Define the marker’s complete lifecycle. `begin_export` must resolve or refuse an existing unresolved export before recording another destination. Entry deletion and expiry must resolve the export or preserve durable unresolved-cleanup information before removing its marker/key, without preventing the practitioner’s audio deletion. Specify `recover_exports` ordering before keyless reconciliation and retention destruction, and retain recovery state when cleanup fails. Add tests for failed cleanup followed by another destination, Delete now, expiry, and restart.
- /fix decision: Apply (plan amendment, verified against the code by the owning planning session, 2026-10-07)
- /fix notes: Confirmed: the round-5 marker lived inside the entry, which Delete now (`past_sessions.py:716-717, 952-957`) and `reconcile_pending` (L628) `rmtree`, and a second export would have overwritten it. Task 3.3, D10 and the test list now specify an export LEDGER outside every entry (`past_sessions\exports.enc` under its own DPAPI-wrapped key), `begin_export` resolving or refusing on an unresolved row, `recover_exports()` running FIRST at start-up, keeping undeletable rows with one content-free line each, and resetting an unreadable ledger with one line; Delete now and expiry never touch it.

**PR-MED-062 — Completion-audit recovery skips entries committed immediately before interruption**

- Plan section: D15; Tasks 1.4 and 2.2.
- Materiality: build-affecting
- Why it matters: Kill the app after `keep.commit()` succeeds but before `_audit_completion()`. The audio is safely archived, but `pending` is already gone. The planned repair processes only newly reconciled entries, so `recording.kept_at` remains empty permanently. An interruption after recovery’s commit but before its audit update has the same result. Task 1.4’s later deletion update depends on `kept_at`, so that deletion fact can also remain empty.
- Current plan text: `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:803`:

- Evidence: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/session_store.py:1371` commits before returning completion facts:

  `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:579`:

  But reconciliation skips an already committed entry at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:630–631`:

  ```python
      continue
  ```
- Suggested change: Extend idempotent recording-fact recovery to already committed entries, or retain a separate durable audit-pending state until the fact is recorded. Run that repair before deletion or retention can remove its evidence. Add interruption tests after successful normal commit and successful reconciliation commit, before their respective audit updates, followed by repeated recovery.
- /fix decision: Apply (plan amendment, verified against the code by the owning planning session, 2026-10-07)
- /fix notes: Confirmed: a kill after `keep.commit()` (`session_store.py:1371`) and before `_audit_completion` (`session.py:1303`) leaves a committed entry without `pending` that `reconcile_pending` skips (`past_sessions.py:630-631`). Task 2.2's start-up repair now runs `record_recording_kept` (idempotent) over EVERY committed kept entry, not only the newly reconciled ids, before `tidy_dead_recordings` and the retention sweep; the start-up order is pinned by a test; both interruption shapes are named in the tests.

#### Findings

##### Unstated assumptions

**PR-MED-071 — Export recovery cannot establish ownership from the ledger’s pathname**

- Plan section: D10; Task 3.3.
- Materiality: build-affecting.
- Why it matters: The ledger records a destination before the temporary file is created, but recovery deletes whichever file later occupies that pathname. If `<session id>.wav.part` already exists and the process dies after recording the ledger row but before opening the file, recovery can delete a practitioner-owned file that this export never created. Replacing the partial file or repurposing its folder while the app is stopped creates the same problem. Calling the target “app-owned” does not establish ownership with the specified ledger shape.
- Current plan text: `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:902`: “`begin_export` first RESOLVES any unresolved ledger row (it deletes that row's `<id>.wav.part` if present and drops the row; if the `.part` cannot be deleted it REFUSES the new export with the manual-cleanup line, so an unresolved partial export is never forgotten by a later one)”.
- Evidence: `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:902` specifies “BEFORE any plaintext is written, `store.begin_export(id, folder)` records the destination” and “a tiny JSON map `{session id: folder}`”. That map contains no file identity. The existing archive deletion boundary explicitly recognizes this ownership distinction: `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:945` says “A ``directory`` that is a link is REFUSED (False, untouched)”, enforced at lines 949–950 by `if _is_link(directory):` / `return False`.
- Suggested change: Define an enforceable ownership contract for creation and recovery. Exclusively create the temporary file, refuse pre-existing files, and durably record sufficient file/directory identity before writing plaintext. Recovery must verify that identity before deletion; changed or unresolved identity should preserve the file and produce a manual-cleanup warning. Test interruption before creation, a pre-existing `.part`, replacement after interruption, and a repurposed or redirected destination.
- /fix decision: Apply (plan amendment, verified against the code by the owning planning session, 2026-10-07)
- /fix notes: Confirmed: a `{session id: folder}` row carries no file identity, so recovery would delete whatever file sat at that path. Task 3.3 now creates the `.part` exclusively (a pre-existing file refuses the export), records the created file's identity (file index + device) in the ledger row BEFORE any plaintext is streamed, and deletes — in `begin_export`'s resolve step and in `recover_exports()` — only a file whose identity still matches; a mismatch keeps the file and its row with the manual-cleanup line; a kill between the create and the row leaves an empty, plaintext-free file. Tests for the pre-existing file, the replaced file and the interruption before creation are named.

##### Missing verification / rollback / migration

**PR-MED-072 — The rollback gate leaves interrupted plaintext exports without recovery**

- Plan section: C5; D14; Tasks 3.3 and 5.3b.
- Materiality: build-affecting.
- Why it matters: Export operates on an already completed entry. A hard kill during export can therefore leave a plaintext `.part` and its ledger row while there are no live sessions. The practitioner can satisfy the stated rollback rule and install 0.2.0, which neither processes the ledger nor warns about the partial file. Its Delete now removes the encrypted entry but leaves that external plaintext behind. The accepted completed-WAV handoff does not cover this interrupted-export state, for which the plan promises recovery.
- Current plan text: `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:916`: “hence finish or discard live sessions before any older install; `development.json` is ignored.” C5 at line 185 promises “an interrupted export recovered at the next start (Task 3.3).”
- Evidence: Current startup at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/app.py:426` calls `past_sessions.clean_staging()`, and line 433 calls `committed = past_sessions.reconcile_pending(sessions_root)`. Neither handles exports. The old archive enumeration at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:824` admits only `if _SESSION_ID_RE.fullmatch(child.name) and not _is_link(child) and child.is_dir():`; the new root-level ledger and key are ignored. Task 3.3 at `.cursor/plans/plan-development-recordings.md:902` introduces the missing behavior: “at start-up `store.recover_exports()` runs FIRST”.
- Suggested change: Extend the rollback gate to resolve outstanding exports before installing an older build: restart 0.3.0 for recovery and resolve any remaining partial files manually, with an explicit fallback when 0.3.0 cannot run. State that 0.2.0 ignores `exports.enc` and `exports-key.dpapi`. Add verification for an interrupted export with no live sessions, including an undeletable partial file; finishing live sessions alone must not satisfy the gate.
- /fix decision: Apply (plan amendment, verified against the code by the owning planning session, 2026-10-07)
- /fix notes: Confirmed: an interrupted export leaves a `.part` and a ledger row with no live session, `app.py:426-433` runs no export recovery today, and 0.2.0 would ignore the root-level ledger. Task 5.3b, D14 and the Config section now make the rollback gate two steps — finish or discard live sessions, THEN start 0.3.0 once more so its start-up resolves any interrupted export (a manual search for `<session id>.wav.part` files if 0.3.0 cannot run) — and state that 0.2.0 ignores `exports.enc` and `exports-key.dpapi`.

#### Findings

- **[MED]** MED-001 `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/main_window.py:2073` — the kept sentence is shown only on the tab the window leaves
  - Triage: Fix-now
  - Fix route: fix-on-fast
  - Why it matters: D2 relies on the Complete saying the recording was kept, so a mis-kept one is noticed and deleted; `_show_completed` appended `COMPLETE_KEPT_LINE` to the Transcript tab's line, but `_on_transcript_closed` then switches to the Session tab (unless phrases were dropped), whose `written_done` notice — or, for an ordinary Complete, no line at all — said nothing; smoke step 5 would have failed as written.
  - Current behaviour: the kept sentence lives on the Transcript tab only.
  - Desired behaviour: the landing Session tab says it too, for `completed` and `written`, from the actual outcome; never on a Discard.
  - Pattern to follow: the `written_done` notice already shown there (draft-write D6).
  - Invariant: the sentence is read from `last_completion_kept()` only for the `completed` / `written` outcomes (a Discard never reads a stale answer).
  - Verification: `test_ui_screens.py::TestKeptLineWhereTheWindowLands` (completed / written / discarded × kept / not kept, the landing tab's text); `test_ui_encounter.py::TestRecoveredKept` (both labels on the recovered path).
  - Regression risk: `_on_transcript_closed`'s other callers' outcomes; `FakeController` is the only test controller with `MainWindow`.
  - /fix decision: Applied
  - /fix notes: `_on_transcript_closed` computes `kept` for `completed` / `written` and appends `COMPLETE_KEPT_LINE` to the `written_done` notice or shows it alone.
  - /fix date: 2026-10-07
  - /fix applied by: Claude Code
- **[LOW]** LOW-001: `past_sessions.py` `delete_recording` — the in-place overwrite (the archive's only write THROUGH an existing file) followed a symlink or hard link and zeroed `st_size` bytes unbounded (a `MemoryError` escaped unmapped) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: refuses unless `link_state(key) is False`, and after opening unless a regular file with `st_nlink == 1` and `0 < size <= AUDIO_KEY_FILE_BYTES` → `recording_delete_failed`, nothing written; tests for a hard link to another entry's key (victim intact and readable), a linked/uninspectable key, an oversized key. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-002: `docs/design-system.md:357` and the tests — the design-system still quoted the old Discard wording ("the recording is kept"), and every test compared against the constant itself, so the old wording could return unnoticed — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: the quote and its lead-in updated ("kept" reserved for development); `test_the_stalled_discard_line_never_says_kept` pins the literal and "kept" absent. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-003: `app.py` `repair_kept_facts` — at every start, after `audit.prune()`, a kept entry older than the audit's 7-year window ("Until I delete them") had its pruned row re-made as `pre_audit` (and shown in a CSV export until the next prune) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: new `AuditLog.keeps_rows_of(at)` (False once the local month's `month_prune_at` has passed; never raises, False on error); the repair skips such entries; pinned by `test_the_repair_never_remakes_a_pruned_row` (real audit) and the `test_audit.py` start-up order test. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-004: `app.py` / `past_sessions.py` `tidy_dead_recordings` — a Delete recording killed after its zeros synced (or whose unlink failed and audit write failed) left the zeroed key as the only evidence, and start-up tidy removed it while the repair recorded only `kept_at` — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: `PastSessionStore.deleted_recordings()` (committed entries with a CONFIRMED zeroed key, or a confirmed-absent key with `audio.enc` confirmed present; labels decrypted only for these; shares `_labelled_where` with `kept_entries`); the start-up repair records kept → `archived` → `record_recording_deleted` (dated when found) for each, BEFORE tidy; a tick-time tidy after a failed click-time audit write stays the named residue (the click records `deleted_at` either way, Task 3.2); pinned by `test_a_deleted_untidied_recording_is_recorded_before_tidy` (zeroed and key-gone, idempotent). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.

#### Findings

- **[MED]** MED-001 `desktop/tests/test_past_sessions.py` `test_delete_recording_refuses_a_linked_or_uninspectable_key` — the symlink half of the `delete_recording` guard was never tested
  - Triage: Fix-now
  - Fix route: fix-on-fast
  - Why it matters: the monkeypatched `link_state` only answered None; a guard regressed to `is None` would pass every test, and a symlinked `audio-key.enc` would be opened `r+b` and its TARGET (a regular one-link file to `fstat`) zeroed; the hard-link test exercises only `st_nlink`.
  - Current behaviour: only "cannot tell" is pinned.
  - Desired behaviour: both True (a link) and None are refused with the key bytes unchanged.
  - Pattern to follow: the module's `link_state` contract (H3 round 35 SEC-001).
  - Verification: the test parametrized over `[True, None]`.
  - Regression risk: none (test only).
  - /fix decision: Applied
  - /fix notes: parametrized (`a_link`, `uninspectable`).
  - /fix date: 2026-10-07
  - /fix applied by: Claude Code
- **[LOW]** LOW-001: `app.py` `repair_kept_facts` (round 11 LOW-003's class) — the window was judged by the label's COMPLETION, but `begin` files the row in the month the session STARTED: a session started 31 Aug and completed 1 Sep had its row re-made for up to a month after August's prune — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: new `_audit_moment(label)` (the start via `past_sessions_view.started_epoch`, else the completion) judges `keeps_rows_of` and dates any re-made row in both the kept repair and the deletion record; `test_the_window_is_judged_by_the_session_start` (real audit, 31 Aug / 1 Sep 2019 against NOW 1 Oct 2026) and a start-in-a-pruned-month entry in the `test_audit.py` order test. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-002: `app.py:453` (round 11 LOW-004's class) — tidy ran after a repair that SKIPPED a deleted recording (label unreadable, or an audit write refused — `update`'s False ignored), and on every tick with no deletion record at all, destroying the only evidence — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: the deletion half is now `record_deleted_recordings` (start-up AND every tick; decrypts only deleted entries' labels), returning the ids it could not record; `tidy_dead_recordings(spare=)` leaves those for the next run (no audit → spares nothing); `test_a_sweep_spares_a_deletion_it_could_not_record` (a refusing then working audit), the `test_audit.py` order test (`tidy` given the unreadable-label id). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-003: `past_sessions.py` `delete_recording` (latent until Task 3.2) — the API forced destroy-then-record: a kill after the unlinks and before the caller's audit write lost the deletion for ever — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: `delete_recording(..., on_destroyed=)` runs the caller's record between the verified zeros and the unlinks; a record that returns False or raises leaves the zeroed key and `audio.enc` as evidence for the next deletion record (the deletion itself stands, nothing raised); two tests. Task 3.2's caller passes its `record_recording_deleted`. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-004: `ui/past_sessions.py:551` — Delete now of a kept entry whose label cannot be read skipped the kept fact, so `deleted_early` (which needs `kept_at`) recorded no `deleted_at` when the Complete's own write had failed — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: such an entry records `record_recording_deleted` (no `kept_at` precondition) before `deleted_early`; `test_an_unreadable_kept_entry_records_its_recording_deletion` (with and without audio). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-005: `past_sessions.py` `_recording_deleted_in` (round 11 LOW-001's class) — `os.lstat` counted a dangling symlink `audio.enc` as present while tidy (`_exists`) did not, so a tampered entry was recorded kept-and-deleted at every start — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: only a REGULAR `audio.enc` (`S_ISREG` from `lstat`) is deletion evidence; `test_a_stray_non_file_audio_is_never_a_deleted_recording` (a folder in its place). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-006: `ui/main_window.py` `_on_transcript_closed` (round 11 MED-001's class) — the kept sentence stayed on the Session tab after a later Complete that kept nothing (a recovered session), reading as that one's — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: a not-kept `completed` clears the line when, and only when, it is still `COMPLETE_KEPT_LINE`; `test_an_earlier_kept_sentence_never_reads_as_a_later_completes` (another notice is left alone). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-007: `ui/bridge.py` / `ui/session_screen.py` (MED-001's class) — the bridge cleared an armed tick silently (Chrome moved, a refused Start, the link dropped); the likely trap: tick armed, THEN the note opened in Chrome → every Start not kept, unannounced — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: `disarm_development_tick(announce=True)` from the bridge's three clearings and the lock/suspend clearing shows `models.DEVELOPMENT_TICK_CLEARED_LINE` on the Session tab when a tick WAS armed (a Start that ran, and the Status setting turned off, announce nothing); pinned in `TestDevelopmentTick` (refusals, moves, the disconnect, a Start that ran). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-008: `ui/main_window.py` `pause_for` (round 11 LOW-005's class) — a tick armed at IDLE survived a lock or suspend, so after lunch the next patient's desktop Start could consume it — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: `MainWindow.pause_for` disarms (announced) for `SYSTEM_REASONS` before delegating; the hotkey and spoken pause do not; `test_a_lock_or_suspend_clears_an_armed_tick_and_says_so`. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.

Rationale: D2 and Task 2.3 define the bound target as "the treatment-note and patient ids only"; Cliniko's ids repeating across the practitioner's two clinics is improbable; a Chrome Start against a removed clinic is refused (which disarms, announced); a desktop Start stays under the clinician's consent tick with the armed line showing.

### Round 13 - 2026-10-07 - Phase 2 re-review attacking rounds 11–12's classes, `/review-loop` round 3 of cap 3 (stage-2 executor)

- Round status: Converged (0 CRIT / 0 HIGH / 0 MED; 12 applied, 3 accepted; 0 pending) — the fixes' verification (the FULL desktop suite) is composer-run, requested 2026-10-07T17:35
- Source: Claude Code (`claude-opus-5-5`, `/execute-loop` run stage-2 executor; three parallel lens subagents — backend classes, UI classes, post-fix regression + the weak-pin class — merged, deduped and verified in-session)
- Review baseline: `0cf9d5b` + the uncommitted Phase 2 working tree after round 12's fixes (targeted selection 1358 passed / 9 skipped, composer-run 2026-10-07 ~16:38)
- Classification: all 🆕 LOWs at new sites of rounds 11–12's classes; none fix-induced except LOW-009 (round 12's `_audit_moment` round trip) and LOW-006 (round 12's stale-line clear incomplete), both narrowings of the round-12 code; round 12's other fixes re-verified clean
- Candidates: 15 raw (incl. 2 doc nits); 1 cross-lens duplicate merged (the incomplete stale clear, UI lens 1 + test lens 2), the doc nits folded into LOW-015 → 15 findings; none dropped

#### Findings

- **[LOW]** LOW-001: `ui/past_sessions.py:542`, `past_sessions.py` `sweep_report` (class 3) — Delete now and the retention sweep asked only `recording_kept` (a LIVE key), so a deletion whose record failed and was spared (zeroed key) was lost with the entry: no kept fact, so `deleted_early` / `expired` set no `deleted_at` — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: new `_recording_held_in` / `PastSessionStore.recording_held` (kept OR deleted-not-tidied) used by both; `test_a_deletion_not_yet_tidied_still_counts_as_held`. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-002: `ui/past_sessions.py:555`, `ui/past_sessions_view.py:537` (class 2) — Delete now's and the retention sweep's added kept-fact writes are not gated by `keeps_rows_of`, and the sweep dates a re-made row by completion — Triage: Accept; Decision: Accepted (executor gate). Rationale: the pre-existing `deleted_early` / `expired` write each sits beside (privacy-professional-controls round 16 LOW-005's pattern) already re-makes that row in exactly these cases, so gating only Phase 2's write changes no outcome; a consistent gate spans the privacy plan's behaviour (out of this plan's scope); under "7 years" the sweep expires before the start month's prune in all but a computer-off edge.
- **[LOW]** LOW-003: `app.py` `record_deleted_recordings` (class 3, cost) — a stuck deleted-not-tidied entry costs an entry-key unwrap and up to three no-op audit updates every 15-minute tick — Triage: Accept; Decision: Accepted (executor gate). Rationale: LOW-012 removes the unreadable-label case (now recorded once, not retried); what remains needs a persistent file lock on a zeroed recording's files — rare, bounded to a handful of entries, silent (DPAPI never prompts), and each no-op update writes nothing.
- **[LOW]** LOW-004: `past_sessions.py` `_audio_key_state` (class 1) — it opened the key file through a link with no regular-file / `st_nlink` / size check, and read an EMPTY file as "zeroed" (a recorded deletion nobody made) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: `link_state` first, then `fstat` on the handle: a link, a non-regular file, `st_nlink != 1` or a size outside 1–60 bytes is `unknown` (decides nothing; shown as kept so the entry can go), "zeroed" only for 1–60 all-zero bytes — the rule `delete_recording` writes under; `test_only_the_apps_own_key_file_decides` (empty, hard-linked). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-005: `test_exclusions.py` `FakeAudit`, `test_hands_free.py` `_InertAudit`, `test_past_sessions.py` `Recorder` — three audit fakes reaching `sweep_with_archive` lacked `keeps_rows_of` (round 8 LOW-004's class; mypy cannot see it through the concrete `AuditLog` type) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-006: `ui/main_window.py` `_on_transcript_closed` (class A; round 12 LOW-006 incomplete) — the stale clear matched only the bare sentence on a `completed` close: "`written_done` + kept" survived a later not-kept Complete, and a Discard left the sentence up — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: any close that kept nothing (Discard included) clears a Session-tab message ENDING in `COMPLETE_KEPT_LINE`, only that; `test_a_kept_written_sentence_is_cleared_by_the_next_close` (completed, discarded). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-007: `ui/session_screen.py` (class A; round 12 LOW-007's line) — the cleared-tick line went to the shared message line, where the next message ("Recording.", a Complete line) replaced it before it was seen — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: a `_tick_cleared` flag renders `DEVELOPMENT_TICK_CLEARED_LINE` on the TRACKED line (`development_label`) until the tick is re-armed, a Start runs, or the setting goes off; the message line is untouched; bridge and lock tests now read the tracked line (one proves a later message does not replace it). Residue: a Start that runs right after a clearing retires the line — the absent "This recording is being kept" line and the absent Complete sentence are the cue. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-008: `ui/session_screen.py:305` (class B) — a failed read of `development.json` during a refresh clears an armed tick unannounced — Triage: Accept; Decision: Accepted (executor gate). Rationale: unreadable reads OFF by design (D1, C3 — the inverse of shadow's polarity), which also HIDES the box, so the tick visibly disappears; the Status tab names an unreadable file when it reads it; announcing would need the tracked line the hidden state does not show.
- **[LOW]** LOW-009: `app.py` `_audit_moment` (never-raise; round 12 fix) — `datetime.fromtimestamp(epoch, UTC)` raises on Windows outside 1970–3000, so a label with a wild date would make start-up raise every time — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: the label's own aware `started_at` (else `completed_at`) is the moment, `started_epoch` only the `created_at`; the dead `epoch is None` branch went. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-010: `test_ui_bridge.py` `test_a_chrome_start_consumes_the_armed_tick` (weak pin) — `message_label != CLEARED` after a Start that ran could not fail ("Recording." always overwrites) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: a spy on `disarm_development_tick` asserts no announced clearing of an armed tick during the Start. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-011: `ui/session_screen.py` `start_linked` / `on_start` (class A) — a Start that read an armed tick and then failed (the start guard, no device, the controller), or met a discard under way or no consent, cleared it unannounced (the bridge's `_refuse("start","failed")` then found nothing armed) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: `_announce_unused_tick(keep, started)` after `_start`, and `announce=True` on the discarding and no-consent clearings. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-012: `app.py` `record_deleted_recordings` — an unreadable label was spared for ever (and retried every tick) while Delete now records the deletion alone for the same case — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: the deletion alone is recorded (`record_recording_deleted`, no dates; spared only if that write is refused), as Delete now does; pinned in the `test_audit.py` order test (a refused write → spared). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-013: `test_audit.py` / `test_past_sessions.py` (weak pin) — "re-made rows are dated by the session's start" (round 12 LOW-001) was not pinned: the fakes dropped `created_at` and the one pinned value had start = completion — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix notes: the order test's deleted label starts the month before it completes and pins the start epoch; `test_a_remade_row_is_filed_in_the_month_the_session_started` (real audit: `pre_audit`, `session_date` 31 Aug, `kept_at` the completion). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-014: `test_integration_no_sockets.py:412` — the child script mirrors `app.main`'s start-up but ran `sweep_with_archive` without `repair_kept=True`, so the repair's label decryption was outside the socket guard — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.
- **[LOW]** LOW-015: docs — `disarm_development_tick`'s docstring (lock/suspend and the Start-failure clearings now announce), the `test_audit.py` order comment (the deletion-record step) — Triage: Fix-now; Decision: Applied. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code.

### Round 14 - 2026-10-07 - Phase 2 (Tasks 2.1–2.4), independent cross-family codex peer-review (pass stage-2.p1, peer round 1 of cap 5)

- Round status: Closed by the stage-2 executor 2026-10-07 (3 applied, 1 invalid; 0 pending) — the fixes' verification (targeted pytest) is composer-run, requested 2026-10-07T17:30; a confirmation peer round follows
- Source: Codex peer-review (`gpt-6-astra`, reasoning medium, read-only sandbox; three file-scoped slices; composer-recorded from `.cursor/loops/stage-2-peer-r14A.log`, `stage-2-peer-r14B.log` and `stage-2-peer-r14C.log` by the `/execute-loop` composer; escape check clean, refs unchanged)
- Review baseline: `0cf9d5b` + the uncommitted Phase 2 working tree (after review rounds 11–13's fixes; full desktop suite 6591 passed / 9 skipped, composer-run)
- Slice A (the archive sidecar, the store's kept-recording reads and deletions, the audit facts, the start-up order): `past_sessions.py`, `session_store.py`, `audit.py`, `app.py`; tests `test_past_sessions.py`, `test_audit.py`, `test_exclusions.py`, `test_integration_no_sockets.py` — 2 claims checked, 2 confirmed, 0 dropped
- Slice B (the Complete funnel, the kept decision, recovery, the Past-sessions destroyers): `session.py`, `ui/{main_window,models,past_sessions,past_sessions_view}.py`; tests `test_session_machine.py`, `test_shadow_exits.py`, `test_ui_encounter.py` — 1 claim checked, 1 confirmed, 0 dropped
- Slice C (the Status-tab setting, the Session-tab development tick, the bridge's disarm, the wording): `ui/{session_screen,bridge,transcript}.py`, `docs/design-system.md`; tests `test_ui_screens.py`, `test_ui_bridge.py`, `test_hands_free.py`, `test_development_settings.py` — 1 claim checked, 1 confirmed, 0 dropped
- Peer-ID map (journal grammar): A01 → PR-MED-001, A02 → PR-MED-002, B01 → PR-MED-003, C01 → PR-MED-004. Composer note on PR-MED-004: the slice-C prompt's job list named "when the link drops" among the clearing cases (the composer's paraphrase of round 11's applied fix); plan D2 itself does not list link loss — the executor verifies against D2 and the code.

#### Findings

- **PR-MED-001** (slice A01) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/app.py:511`): Deletion repair can recreate a pruned audit row when the archive label is unreadable. This branch bypasses the retention check and creates a new row dated today, extending retention another seven years. — Evidence: “`if label is None:`” then “`audit.record_recording_deleted(session_id)`”; `audit.py:1128–1131` creates a missing row using “`session_date=_date_from_epoch(created_at, now, self._zone)`”. Recommendation: Fix-now — For undated entries, update existing rows without creating missing ones; test an unreadable label after audit pruning. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed: round 13 LOW-012's unreadable-label branch calls `record_recording_deleted(session_id)`, and `AuditLog._update` makes a `pre_audit` row dated now when none exists, with no date to judge the window by. /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: `AuditLog.update(..., create=)` / `_update(..., create=)` and `record_recording_deleted(..., create=)` — with `create=False` a session with no row is left without one (nothing written, True returned); `app.record_deleted_recordings` passes `create=False` for an unreadable label; `test_an_unreadable_labels_deletion_never_remakes_a_row` (real audit: an existing row gains `deleted_at`; a pruned one is not re-made; the evidence is tidied either way) and the `test_audit.py` order test (`{"create": False}`). Delete now's own unreadable-label call is unchanged: its `deleted_early` write beside it already re-makes that row (the pre-existing pattern, round 13 LOW-002). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **PR-MED-002** (slice A02) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/app.py:457`): Failed deletion recording preserves evidence only against tidy; the subsequent retention sweep can destroy that same evidence permanently. An expired entry with a zeroed audio key and an unavailable audit survives tidy but is deleted by retention, leaving nothing for the next repair. — Evidence: “`past_sessions.tidy_dead_recordings(spare=unrecorded)`”; `app.py:814` subsequently calls “`window.past_sessions_screen.run_retention_sweep()`”; `past_sessions.py:1030` calls “`self.delete_entry(session_id)`”. The confirmed caller, `ui/past_sessions_view.py:532–538`, records audit facts only after “`report = store.sweep_report(retention_days, now)`”. Recommendation: Fix-now — Preserve unresolved deletion evidence through retention too, and test a failed audit write followed by retention in the same startup. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `tidy_dead_recordings(spare=)` held the entry from tidy only; the start-up retention sweep (`app.main` → `run_retention_sweep`) could expire it in the same start-up. /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: the store remembers the latest spare set (`PastSessionStore._spared`, set by every `tidy_dead_recordings` call) and `sweep_report` HOLDS a due entry in it — counted as not deleted (`failed`), so the tab says something due could not be deleted — until a later sweep records the deletion and stops sparing it; this delays an expiry (retention is a minimum) and never makes an audit call refuse anything (only `begin` may); with a working audit the round-14 PR-MED-003 reorder also records the kept fact before the entry goes; `test_a_spared_deletion_is_held_from_the_retention_sweep` (a refused audit then the sweep: held; a working audit: tidied, then expired). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **PR-MED-003** (slice B01) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/past_sessions.py:544`): Delete now and retention expiry destroy the entry before persisting its kept fact or deletion outcome. An interruption in this gap permanently removes the evidence needed for audit repair, violating the required ordering. — Evidence: `store.delete_entry(session_id)` precedes `self._audit.record_recording_kept(...)` at lines 555–557 and `record_past_session(...)` at line 563. Likewise, `ui/past_sessions_view.py:532` calls `report = store.sweep_report(retention_days, now)` before either audit call at lines 537–538; the callee deletes entries at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:1030`. Recommendation: Fix-now — Persist the kept fact before destruction, preserve recoverable deletion accounting across interruption, and pin this ordering in tests. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed for the KEPT FACT (Phase 2's write): it is true whether or not the deletion succeeds, so it can and should precede the destruction; the OUTCOME writes (`deleted_early`, `expired`) stay after the deletion — they are true only once the entry is gone (recording one first would be false on a failed deletion), and their post-deletion gap is the privacy plan's pre-existing ordering, unchanged here. /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: Delete now records `record_recording_kept` BEFORE `delete_entry` (held entries with a readable label); `sweep_report(..., before_held_delete=)` calls the caller's kept-fact record just before deleting a held entry (whatever it raises is logged — `kept_record_failed` — and the deletion goes ahead, C2), and `past_sessions_view.retention_sweep` passes it (`_record_kept`, via `functools.partial`) instead of recording after the report; pinned by the entry's `key.dpapi` still present at the record's moment (`test_the_retention_sweep_reports_which_expired_entries_were_kept`; `test_delete_now_records_a_kept_recording_first`) and `test_a_failing_kept_record_never_blocks_the_expiry`. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **PR-MED-004** (slice C01) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/bridge.py:478`): Link loss leaves the development tick armed when it was checked without a reported Chrome note, contrary to this round’s unconditional link-drop clearing requirement. A later desktop Start can consume that stale tick. — Evidence: `if self._screen.development_target is not None:` gates `disarm_development_tick(announce=True)`. `desktop/tests/test_ui_bridge.py:1252–1254` explicitly disconnects and then asserts `h.screen.development_checkbox.isChecked()`. Recommendation: Fix-now — Disarm any armed tick on disconnect, and change the test to require clearing, its notice, and no development consent on the next Start without re-arming. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=invalid — D2 binds the tick to "the note/patient ids bound when the tick was armed" and clears it when Chrome's reported context DIFFERS from them; it lists no link-loss case (round 11 LOW-005's disarm is for a tick bound to a Chrome note, whose context the dropped link took away). A tick armed with no note reported has nothing bound to lose: it is a desktop arm for a desktop Start, and the first note report after a reconnect differs from "none" and clears it (announced). `test_ui_bridge.py:1252` pins that intended behaviour. /fix decision: Not reproducible (invalid against D2). /fix notes: no change. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).

PEER-ROUND-14 RESULT: 4 findings (CRIT 0 / HIGH 0 / MED 4 / LOW 0) — slice A 2, slice B 1, slice C 1.

- Slice B (the Complete funnel, the kept decision, recovery, the Past-sessions destroyers): `session.py`, `ui/{main_window,models,past_sessions,past_sessions_view}.py`; tests `test_session_machine.py`, `test_shadow_exits.py`, `test_ui_encounter.py` — 3 claims checked, 2 confirmed, 1 dropped

Attack-the-class re-review of round 14's classes R14-1 (A01, B01) and R14-2 (A02/B02); slice C confirmed none.

#### Findings

- **PR-MED-001** (slice A01) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/app.py:453`): Reconciliation bypasses the retention-safe repair: a pending entry whose source key is absent can recreate a pruned audit row dated today. A missing recent row is likewise assigned the wrong month before the subsequent repair runs. — Evidence: “`audit.record_past_session(session_id, "archived")`” supplies neither a date nor a creation restriction; `audit.py:1143` creates the row with “`session_date=_date_from_epoch(created_at, now, self._zone)`”. Recommendation: Fix-now — Apply the label-based retention/date checks to reconciliation too; use existing-row-only updates when no date is available, and test pending entries with missing and pruned rows. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed: `sweep_with_archive` called `record_past_session(id, "archived")` with no date and no restriction, so `AuditLog._update` made a missing row `pre_audit` dated now — re-made after the prune (a pending entry outlives its 24 h only when the app was not started since, or the clock jumped) or filed in today's month after a D9 reset (the dated repair then finds it there); the privacy plan's residue (p) named "now for a reconciled commit". /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: `record_past_session(..., create=)` (as `record_recording_deleted`); `PastSessionStore.entry_label(id)` (the committed entry's label, never raises); `app.record_reconciled_commit` — the repair's rule: judged by `keeps_rows_of` and dated by the start (`_audit_moment`), `create=False` with no readable label; only the just-committed entries' labels are decrypted; threat-model residue (p) reworded; `test_a_reconciled_commit_is_dated_by_its_label` (real audit: reset → `pre_audit` in the start month; pruned → none; unreadable → the existing row only, none re-made) and the `test_audit.py` start-up order test (a dated, a skipped-pruned and a `create=False` commit). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **PR-MED-002** (slice A02) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:969`): Delete now bypasses the new preservation of failed deletion evidence. An entry spared after an audit failure can still lose its entry key and zeroed sidecar while the audit remains unavailable, permanently preventing repair. — Evidence: `delete_entry` proceeds directly from “`entry = self._committed(session_id)`” to “`if not _remove_key_first(entry):`”; the “`session_id in self._spared`” guard exists only in `sweep_report` at line 1049. The confirmed caller, `ui/past_sessions.py:551–553`, ignores the audit write’s result before “`store.delete_entry(session_id)`”. The class was attacked anyway (/fix applied as hardening). /fix decision: Accepted (re-graded LOW, executor gate), with the class hardening applied. /fix notes: Delete now records a deletion that ALREADY happened (`recording_held` and not `recording_kept`) BEFORE `delete_entry` (`record_recording_deleted`, dated by the start) — a refused write never holds it; the retention sweep judges a gone recording AFRESH instead of only from the latest tidy's spare set: `before_held_delete(id, completed, recording_gone)` also records the deletion (`_record_kept`), and an entry whose deletion it did not record is HELD (counted not deleted) — a live recording's failed kept fact still never blocks an expiry; every other destroyer checked: `remove_pending_entry` and the sweep's `before_destroy` touch only uncommitted entries, `reconcile_pending`'s `rmtree` only keyless ones, `tidy_dead_recordings` honours the spare set, and `delete_recording` refuses a gone recording (`recording_not_kept`); `test_delete_now_records_an_untidied_deletion_before_the_entry_goes` (readable and unreadable label; the entry present at the record; a refused write still deletes), `test_an_unrecordable_deletion_is_held_from_the_retention_sweep` (refused and raising; the live one expires; recorded → expired), and the round-13 held test now pins kept → deleted → expired. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).

Round 14’s fix notes explicitly confirm this recreation remains. #### Findings

- **PR-MED-001** (slice A01) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:1076`): The new retention callback bypasses the retention-safe repair rule: it can recreate a pruned audit row and dates missing rows by completion rather than session start. This is an unattended sweep, outside the accepted deliberate-Delete-now exception. — Evidence: `recorded = before_held_delete(session_id, completed, recording_gone)`; the confirmed caller at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/past_sessions_view.py:520–524` uses `created_at = completed.timestamp()` and calls `audit.record_recording_kept(...)` and `audit.record_recording_deleted(...)` without checking `keeps_rows_of`; `audit.py:1148–1150` creates the missing row. Recommendation: Fix-now — Apply the label-start retention check to the callback and subsequent expiry write, using `create=False` when undated; test startup repair followed by retention with a pruned row and a month-crossing label. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed: round 15's `_record_kept` (the sweep's `before_held_delete`) wrote the kept fact and a gone recording's deletion with `created_at = completed.timestamp()` and no `keeps_rows_of`, and the `expired` write beside it likewise — an unattended writer outside the rule rounds 11/14/15 set for the others (round 13 LOW-002 had accepted the kept write's form as matching the privacy plan's `expired`; round 15 made the rule explicit, so this site now breaks it). The `expired` outcome is judged UNATTENDED, not a deliberate event like Delete now's `deleted_early`: an expiry is the scheduled end of the retention minimum, and an entry whose start month the prune has passed has reached the end of the audit's own seven years with it (`month_prune_at` = the month's end + 7 years + 1 day; the "7 years" retention is 2557 days from completion), so re-making its row would extend a content-free record past its schedule with no accountability gain — whereas Delete now is an EARLY destruction the audit exists to show; the expiring entry's label is always readable (the sweep dates it), so the gate never meets an undated case and no Accept is needed. /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: ONE shared rule, `ui/past_sessions_view.unattended_write(audit, moment) -> UnattendedWrite(created_at, create) | None` (no moment → existing row only; a pruned month → write nothing; else dated by the moment; never raises) with `audit_moment(label)` (start, else completion), used by EVERY unattended writer — `app.record_reconciled_commit`, `repair_kept_facts`, `record_deleted_recordings` (`app._audit_moment` removed) and the retention sweep (`_record_kept` and the `expired` write); the store caches each dated entry's start beside its completion (`_started_dates`, the same lifetime) and reports it (`RetentionSweepReport.started`; `before_held_delete(id, completed, started, recording_gone)`); a pruned row owes nothing, so a gone recording there is not held; `record_recording_kept(..., create=)` and `PastSessionsAudit` gains `keeps_rows_of` and the `create` keywords (both pinned doubles updated); threat-model residue (p) states the one rule; Task 3.2 amended (any new unattended writer uses it). Last sweep of the class: the deliberate writers (Delete now; Task 3.2's Delete recording and Export) record their event by design (round 15 PR-MED-003); the live-session writers (`begin`'s session's completion, discard, write records, recovery) touch rows `begin` made for a recent session; the privacy plan's 24 h sweep record (`record_sweep_results`) dates a re-made row by the session's own trusted creation, so a pruned month's row is pruned again at the next prune — no extension, out of this plan's scope. Tests: `test_the_unattended_rule`, `test_the_retention_sweep_follows_the_unattended_rule` (real audit, 2027/2034: reset → `pre_audit` in the start month with kept/deleted facts; month-crossing pruned start → no row, the entry still expires), the UI expiry tests now pin the start, the `test_audit.py` order test pins `created_at`/`create` on every unattended write, and the raising-kept test now proves the raising write is reached. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **PR-MED-002** (slice B01, a duplicate of PR-MED-001) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/ui/past_sessions_view.py:520`): The unattended retention sweep still recreates pruned audit rows without checking the seven-year window and dates them by completion rather than the label’s start, leaving class R14-1/R15-1 unresolved. — Evidence: “`created_at = completed.timestamp()`” followed by “`kept = audit.record_recording_kept(session_id, completed, created_at=created_at)`”; the confirmed callee at `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/audit.py:898` delegates directly to “`self.update(...)`”. The deletion repair at line 524 and expiry outcome at line 559 likewise lack retention checks. Recommendation: Fix-now — Apply the retention-window and label-start rules to all three sweep writes; verify that an expired entry is removed without recreating its pruned audit row. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — a duplicate of PR-MED-001 (the same three sweep writes from the caller's side). /fix decision: Duplicate of PR-MED-001 (applied with it). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).

The unattended-write class (R14-1 / R15-1 / R16-1) produced no finding in any slice.

#### Findings

- **PR-MED-001** (slice A01) (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:941`): A transient read failure during deletion discovery can let tidy destroy unrecorded deletion evidence. If a zeroed audio key is temporarily unreadable, discovery omits the entry; when the subsequent tidy read succeeds, both files are removed without recording the deletion. — Evidence: `_audio_key_state` returns `"unknown"` on `OSError` (1327–1328), and `_recording_deleted_in` returns `False` for that state (1359–1360). `app.py:524` processes only `"for listing in past_sessions.deleted_recordings()"`, then calls `"past_sessions.tidy_dead_recordings(spare=unrecorded)"` (458). Tidy independently rereads the state (941) and executes `"path.unlink()"` (950). Recommendation: Fix-now — Require positive per-entry clearance from deletion recording before tidying committed entries; test a key read failing during discovery and succeeding during tidy. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed: discovery (`deleted_recordings` → `_recording_deleted_in`) read an `OSError` key as `unknown` → not deleted, and tidy re-read the key itself and removed the files of anything not in `spare` (an exclusion list) — so a read that failed then succeeded destroyed unrecorded evidence; the same shape held when the whole listing failed (`_labelled_where` swallows it as `[]`, tidy then walked everything), and Delete now and the retention sweep each decided "held" and "gone" from TWO reads that a transient error could make disagree. /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: POSITIVE CLEARANCE — `app.record_deleted_recordings` returns the CLEARED ids (listed and recorded, or owing nothing because the prune removed the row) and `tidy_dead_recordings(cleared=)` removes a dead recording's files ONLY for an id in it; every other dead recording tidy finds (a refused record, or one discovery missed) is left and held from the retention sweep (`_spared` = found dead and not cleared); `cleared=None` (no audit — never in production, `app.main` always passes one) tidies all; one-read decisions — `_recording_state(entry) -> none|kept|gone` from a single key read, `PastSessionStore.recording_state(id)` used by Delete now, and the sweep's `held_recording` / `recording_gone` from one call (`_recording_kept_in` / `_recording_deleted_in` / `recording_held` now derive from it; `_recording_held_in` removed); a transient unknown read in the sweep or Delete now reads as KEPT, so the kept fact is recorded first and the `expired` / `deleted_early` outcome sets `recording.deleted_at` in the same pass (only an audit outage loses it — C2, as for any kept recording). Class sweep: every destroyer that acts on a re-read state — tidy (now clearance-gated), the retention sweep and Delete now (one read; a failed read errs to recording the facts), `delete_recording` (refuses anything not live, `recording_not_kept`), `reconcile_pending`'s keyless `rmtree` (only `key.dpapi` decides, already confirmed absent), `remove_pending_entry` (uncommitted only). Tests: `test_tidy_needs_positive_clearance` (the key read failing during discovery and succeeding during tidy; the whole listing failing — evidence kept, held from expiry, recorded and tidied on the next sweep), `test_a_destroyer_decides_from_one_read`, and the `test_audit.py` order test pins tidy's clearance set (the recorded and the pruned id, never the refused one). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).

#### Findings

- **PR-LOW-001** (slice A01) (LOW, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py:955`): Tidy permanently holds an otherwise expirable entry when its audio key is absent and `audio.enc` is a directory. Discovery classifies this as no recording, so it never grants clearance; tidy repeatedly adds it to `_spared`, blocking retention even with a healthy audit. — Evidence: discovery returns `"gone" if stat.S_ISREG(status.st_mode) else "none"` (1369), but tidy uses `"paths = [entry / name for name in names if _exists(entry / name)]"` and `"held.add(entry.name)"` (955–959); retention then skips `"if session_id in self._spared"` (1094). `desktop/tests/test_past_sessions.py:1917` already constructs this non-file case but checks only discovery. Recommendation: Fix-now — Exclude confirmed non-file artifacts from deletion-evidence holds and extend that test through repeated tidy and retention sweeps. Executor verification: surface=production, rec=Fix-now — confirmed: round 17's tidy chose its dead set by its own rule (key absent or zeroed, then any existing path), while discovery's `_recording_state` calls a non-regular `audio.enc` beside no key "none" — so that entry was never cleared yet held (`_spared`) on every tick, blocking its expiry for ever with a healthy audit (before round 17 tidy only failed to unlink the folder, logged each tick). /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: tidy now decides what is dead by discovery's own rule (`_recording_state(entry) == "gone"`), so the two can never disagree on what a recording is: a non-file `audio.enc` with no key is neither tidied nor held; a zeroed key's files (an `audio.enc` folder beside it is logged `recording_cleanup_pending` once, then reads "none"); `test_a_stray_non_file_audio_is_never_a_deleted_recording` extended through two sweeps with a healthy audit (the folder left alone) and a retention sweep (the entry expires, nothing held). /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).

#### Findings

- **[LOW]** SIMP-001: `past_sessions.py` `_audio_key_state` and `delete_recording` — the "this entry's own key file" check (regular, `st_nlink == 1`, 1–60 bytes; round 13 LOW-004's rule, which the reader's docstring says the writer shares) was spelled out twice — a duplicated invariant that could drift between what is read as a deletion and what is zeroed. Triage: Fix-now. Fix route: `/fix`. Current: two three-condition expressions. Desired: one `_is_own_key_file(status)` used by both. Behaviour-preserving: the negations are exact (De Morgan; `!= 1` ↔ `== 1`), same handle, same order. Pattern siblings: `st_nlink` searched across `src/` — only these two sites. Verification: ruff, mypy; the key-file tests (`test_only_the_apps_own_key_file_decides`, the `delete_recording` link / hard-link / size refusals) in the full suite. Regression risk: low. /fix decision: Applied. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **[LOW]** SIMP-002: `past_sessions.py` `PastSessionStore.recording_kept` — re-implemented `recording_state`'s prologue (validate the id, refuse a link) beside `recording_held`, which already derives from `recording_state`. Triage: Fix-now. Fix route: `/fix`. Desired: `return self.recording_state(session_id) == "kept"` — one canonical path for the three readers. Behaviour-preserving: an invalid id or a link is `"none"` → False, as before; otherwise `_recording_state(entry) == "kept"` is exactly `_recording_kept_in(entry)`. Pattern siblings: `recording_held` (already derived); none other. Verification: ruff, mypy, the `recording_kept` tests in the full suite. Regression risk: low. /fix decision: Applied. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).
- **[LOW]** SIMP-003: `ui/session_screen.py` `on_start` / `start_linked` — both repeated D2's ordering past their refusals (read the development tick, clear both ticks, `_start` with what was read, announce an unused tick) through a single-caller-pair helper `_announce_unused_tick`. Triage: Fix-now. Fix route: `/fix`. Desired: one `_start_consuming_ticks(consent, context) -> bool` holding that sequence (the announce folded in); each Start keeps its own refusals (consent, the hold, a discard under way) and calls it. Behaviour-preserving: the same calls in the same order with the same arguments; `start_linked` returns its result as before, `on_start` discards it as before. Pattern siblings: no third Start path (`_controller.start` is called only from `_start`). Verification: ruff, mypy; the tick tests in `test_ui_screens.py` / `test_ui_bridge.py` (read-then-cleared on both Starts, the unused-tick announcement, the hold keeping both ticks) in the full suite. Regression risk: low. /fix decision: Applied. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-2 executor).

Consciously left alone: `StatusPanel._show_development_setting` / `_on_development_toggled` mirroring the shadow pair (plan Task 2.3 asks for the mirror); the four live Complete paths each spelling `kept=live.session.development_consent is not None` (the counted-site pin in `test_shadow_exits.py` wants every site to name the keyword); `sweep_report`'s two `RetentionSweepReport` returns (the pre-existing shape); `disarm_development_tick`'s two `if armed` tests (taste); `recording_held` kept although only tests call it now (a public read the tests pin).

### Round 20 - 2026-10-07 - Phase 3 (Tasks 3.1–3.4), `/review-loop` round 1 of cap 3 (stage-3 executor)

- Round status: Closed by the stage-3 executor 2026-10-07 (12 applied, 9 no change; 0 pending) — the fixes' verification (targeted pytest) is composer-run, requested 2026-10-07; a MED, so round 21 follows
- Source: Claude Code `/review-loop` (`claude-opus-5-5`, `/execute-loop` run stage-3 executor; 4 foreground lens subagents — correctness/security, judgment/structure, regression/missed issue, plan adherence; every candidate re-read against the code before it was logged)
- Scope: the uncommitted Phase 3 working tree over `756b17f` — `past_sessions.py` (the export ledger, `export_recording`, `recover_exports`), `exclusions.py` (`check_export_location`), `speech.py` (`write_wav`), `validation_set.py`, `install_layout.py`, `session_store.py`, `app.py`, `ui/{main_window,past_sessions,past_sessions_view}.py`, the tests and `docs/design-system.md`
- Review baseline: `756b17f` + the uncommitted Phase 3 working tree (full desktop suite 6684 passed / 9 skipped, composer-run)
- Round classification: n/a (round 1 of this phase's loop). Plan adherence: all 26 Done-when clauses of Tasks 3.1–3.4 met; deviations (a)–(f) of the activation-1 brief judged safe.

#### Findings

- **MED-001** (MED, behavioral, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/past_sessions.py` `_export`): on a failure after the `.part` existed, the `finally` closed the stream unguarded — a close whose own flush fails (a full disk, the usual reason the write failed) raised before `_remove_part`, so a `.part` holding plaintext was left with its row and a raw `OSError` escaped `export_recording` (its contract: `PastSessionError` only). Evidence: the `finally` at L1184 called `stream.close()` before the removal. /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: the whole fill (identity, row, PCM, sync, close, rename) runs under ONE `except BaseException` — the close retried there and its `OSError` swallowed (the handle is closed either way), then the identity-checked removal (or by name when no identity was read), the row dropped only once the file is gone, and anything not a `PastSessionError` mapped to `export_failed` (KeyboardInterrupt/SystemExit re-raised after the clean-up); the `finished` flag and the double row-drop gone. Test: `test_a_close_that_fails_after_a_failure_still_removes_the_part`. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-3 executor).
- **MED-002** (MED, test-coverage, `desktop/tests/test_past_sessions.py`): neither `export_unrecorded` path was pinned — the ledger rewrite after a resolution failing, and the row write before the first chunk failing (the empty `.part` must be removed and no audio written). /fix decision: Applied. Tests: `test_a_row_that_cannot_be_written_writes_no_audio`, `test_a_resolution_that_cannot_be_recorded_refuses`. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-3 executor).
- **MED-003** (MED, security-residue, `desktop/src/scribe_desktop/exclusions.py` `check_export_location`): the fixed-drive rule reads the drive LETTER's type, so an external drive Windows reports as fixed (many USB SSDs) and another volume mounted into a folder of a fixed drive are admitted. Closing it needs the volume path (`GetVolumePathNameW`) and the bus type (`IOCTL_STORAGE_QUERY_PROPERTY`) through the layer — beyond D10's rule as planned (removable = not `DRIVE_FIXED`). /fix decision: Applied (as a named residue — no scope expansion). /fix notes: the function's docstring names both limits; Task 5.3a's "the location check's reach and its limits" now lists them for the threat model. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-3 executor).
- **LOW-001** (`past_sessions.py` `_export`): an `os.fstat` or `ExportIdentity` failure escaped raw (outside the inner handler). /fix decision: Applied — inside MED-001's single handler (removed by name, `export_failed`).
- **LOW-002** (`past_sessions.py` `_remove_part`): "not found" also covers a missing FOLDER (an unplugged drive), so the row is dropped. /fix decision: No change — tried and reverted: a folder deleted by hand would otherwise hold its row, and refuse every later export (`export_unresolved`), for ever; the destination check admits only fixed drives; the docstring now says so and `test_a_deleted_export_folder_drops_its_row` pins it.
- **LOW-003** (`past_sessions.py` `_export`): the ledger stored the folder as given — a relative path would be resolved against whatever the working directory is at the next start. /fix decision: Applied — a relative folder is refused (`export_failed`) before anything is read; `test_a_relative_folder_is_refused`.
- **LOW-004** (`past_sessions.py` `_read_ledger` / `recover_exports`): any read error — a transient lock or scanner included — read as damage, and start-up then RESET the ledger, forgetting every unresolved `.part`; `recover_exports` had a dead `except Exception`. /fix decision: Applied — `_LedgerBusy` (link state unreadable, or the file's bytes could not be read) is distinct from `_LedgerUnreadable` (a link, over the cap, the key, the bytes' shape); busy refuses an export (`export_ledger_busy`, "the record of earlier exports could not be opened just now - try again in a moment") and leaves the ledger as it is at start-up; `test_a_ledger_that_cannot_be_read_this_time_refuses_and_is_never_reset`.
- **LOW-005** (`past_sessions.py` `_export`): the clean-up's control flow (a `finished` flag, a `finally` that raised, two row drops) and the docstring's "keeps its row" when no row had been written. /fix decision: Applied — MED-001's single handler; the docstring names the busy and relative refusals.
- **LOW-006** (`ui/past_sessions.py` `on_export_recording`): an entry whose label cannot be read was asked "This was a shadow recording…" — possibly untrue; and that path (the question, `created_at=None`) was untested. /fix decision: Applied — `past_sessions_view.export_confirm_question(label)` asks "Clinic Scribe cannot tell whether this was a shadow recording; …" for an unreadable label; `test_an_unreadable_label_is_asked_neutrally_and_counted_undated`.
- **LOW-007** (`ui/past_sessions_view.py` `export_recording_failed_line`): the manual clean-up line said only "its name ends .wav.part" — the plan (PR-MED-023) names `<session id>.wav.part`, the only way to find it. /fix decision: Applied — the line takes the session id; the existing `test_a_partial_file_that_cannot_be_removed_is_named` re-pinned (round 21: a duplicate added here was removed).
- **LOW-008** (`tests/test_shadow_exits.py`): the one-writer pin saw `wave.open` only by that name — `import wave as w` or `from wave import open` would slip past. /fix decision: Applied — `_WAVE_IMPORTS` pins where `wave` is imported, unaliased (the two readers and the writer), with three negative cases.
- **LOW-009** (`docs/design-system.md`): "never an id" contradicted the new partial-file lines; the Session-tab tick strings were attributed to `ui/main_window.py` (they live in `ui/models.py`); the bullet omitted the store's refusal line, the ledger-reset line and the unreadable-label question. /fix decision: Applied.
- **LOW-010** (`exclusions.py`): `own =_normalised(` spacing. /fix decision: Applied. (Keeping `DRIVE_FIXED` beside its one user and the drive parsing as it is: no change.)
- **LOW-011** (`past_sessions.py` `_remove_part`): the identity is checked, then the name unlinked — a same-user process could swap the file in between. /fix decision: No change — a same-user residue (the threat model's standing boundary); named for Task 5.3a.
- **LOW-012** (`exclusions.py`): a folder other local accounts can read is admitted. /fix decision: No change — the check is about the file leaving the computer (D10); the same-computer, other-account case is the single-practitioner machine's residue.
- **LOW-013** (`ui/past_sessions.py`): Delete recording's two-click arming duplicates Delete now's. /fix decision: No change — the plan asks for Delete now's pattern; two small state machines on different targets read more plainly than a shared helper.
- **LOW-014** (`ui/past_sessions.py`): the start-up recovery lines show only on the Past sessions tab. /fix decision: No change — Task 3.3 places them on that tab's status line, where Export lives.
- **LOW-015** (plan text): Task 3.3's prose names `begin_export` / `end_export` / `ExportRefusal`. /fix decision: No change — deviation (c) of the activation-1 brief records the as-built names.
- **LOW-016** (`tests/test_validation_set.py` `TestTheSharedWavWriter`): would pass before the change. /fix decision: No change — it pins behaviour (the bytes); the AST pins in `test_shadow_exits.py` are the guard.
- **LOW-017** (`speech.py` `write_wav`): the `Path` branch is used only by a test. /fix decision: No change — the natural signature for a later caller, and the branch is one line.
- **LOW-018** (`past_sessions.py` `PAST_SESSION_REASONS["export_cleanup_failed"]`): never shown (the view has its own line). /fix decision: No change — the fallback text for any other caller of `failure_reason`.

### Round 21 - 2026-10-07 - Phase 3 (Tasks 3.1–3.4), `/review-loop` round 2 of cap 3 — re-review attacking round 20's classes (stage-3 executor)

- Scope: the whole uncommitted Phase 3 working tree over `756b17f`, after round 20's fixes and the composer-reported stale pin (`test_a_partial_file_that_cannot_be_removed_is_named`, re-pinned to the named line before the round)
- Review baseline: `756b17f` + the uncommitted Phase 3 working tree (round 20 selection 1059 passed / 9 skipped / 1 failed — the stale pin — composer-run)
- Round classification: 🆕 new sites of round 20's classes — MED-001 and LOW-004 (pins a different spelling slips past; R20 LOW-008), LOW-001 (transient read treated as damage; R20 LOW-004), LOW-005 (a line that does not name its file; R20 LOW-007), LOW-002 and LOW-003 (failure path / untested branch; R20 MED-001/MED-002), LOW-007 (ambient state stored for later; R20 LOW-003); fix-induced by round 20: LOW-009, LOW-010, LOW-011. Dropped at verification: the KeyboardInterrupt-whose-clean-up-fails edge, the deferred `speech` import before the handler (imported at start-up), a NUL in a DPAPI-authenticated ledger folder, the review-due month/time-zone pair (agree), and stale start-up lines after a later export resolves a row (wording only).

#### Findings

- **MED-001** (MED, test-guard, `desktop/tests/test_shadow_exits.py`): the audio pins counted where a WAV is OPENED, not who turns a kept recording into plaintext — a second caller of `speech.write_wav`, or a plain file write over `read_recording`, would skip Export's location check, ledger and shadow question with every count unchanged (the file's other exits pin their CALLERS). /fix decision: Applied. /fix notes: `_RECORDING_READERS` (`read_recording` called only by `PastSessionStore.export_recording`) and `_WRITE_WAV_USERS` (the writer reached — by any import alias or `<module>.write_wav` attribute — only from `PastSessionStore._export` and `validation_set.py`'s module import), with negative cases for a second reader (UI and store) and three spellings of a second writer caller. /fix date: 2026-10-07. /fix applied by: Claude Code (stage-3 executor).
- **LOW-001** (`past_sessions.py` `_read_ledger`): round 20 made a transient read of `exports.enc` busy, but a transient read of its KEY file (`unwrap_key_from_file` wraps the `OSError` in `KeyCustodyError`) still read as damage — start-up then reset the ledger and forgot every unresolved `.part`. /fix decision: Applied — the unwrap is judged on its own: an `OSError` other than "not found", raw or as the wrapped cause (`_transient`), is `_LedgerBusy`; a missing key or one that does not unwrap stays damage. Tests: `test_a_ledger_key_that_cannot_be_read_this_time_is_busy_too` (raw and wrapped), `test_a_missing_ledger_key_is_damage_and_resets`.
- **LOW-002** (`ui/past_sessions.py` `_delete_recording`): the kept fact's write result was ignored — with Complete's own kept write failed and the UI's failing too, the deletion was recorded and the zeroed files unlinked, leaving a row with `deleted_at` and no `kept_at` that nothing repairs (the unattended `app.record_deleted_recordings` clears only when kept, archived and deleted are all recorded). /fix decision: Applied — `on_destroyed` is `_record_deletion(..., kept_recorded=)`, True only when both facts were recorded, so the zeroed files stay for the next run's full record; `test_an_unrecorded_kept_fact_keeps_the_zeroed_files_for_the_next_run`.
- **LOW-003** (`desktop/tests/test_past_sessions.py`): untested failure branches — `os.fstat` failing (removal by name), `recover_exports`'s ledger rewrite failing after a resolution, `_drop_export_row` failing after a successful rename. /fix decision: Applied — `test_an_identity_that_cannot_be_read_removes_the_part_by_name`, `test_a_recovery_that_cannot_be_recorded_is_resolved_again`, `test_a_row_that_cannot_be_dropped_after_the_rename_is_resolved_later`. (`_reset_ledger`'s partial unlink: no change — it never raises and logs; a key-less ledger then reads as damage and is reset again.)
- **LOW-004** (`tests/test_shadow_exits.py` `_wav_writers`): `speech.wave.open(p, "wb")` and `wave.Wave_write(f)` slipped past the writer count. /fix decision: Applied — any `.open` / `.Wave_write` on `wave` or `<x>.wave` counts; two negative cases. (Rebinding `w = wave` or `getattr(wave, "open")`: no change — deliberate evasion inside the three files allowed to import `wave`, which review reads.)
- **LOW-005** (`ui/past_sessions_view.py`, `past_sessions.py`): only the chosen folder is used, yet the success line named no file, `export_exists` said "a file of that name" (possibly not the name typed), `export_part_exists` and `export_unresolved` named no `.part` (the latter's is an EARLIER export's, possibly another session's), and the save dialog could ask to replace a file nothing replaces. /fix decision: Applied — `recording_exported_line(session_id)` ("Exported as <id>.wav. …"); `export_recording_failed_line` names `<id>.wav` / `<id>.wav.part` for those reasons; `ExportUnresolvedError(PastSessionError)` carries the stuck rows' ids and the line names each `.wav.part`; the dialog passes `DontConfirmOverwrite`. Test: `test_each_refusal_names_the_file_it_is_about`; the unresolved store tests pin `session_ids`.
- **LOW-006** (`ui/past_sessions_view.py` `EXPORT_CHOOSE_LOCAL`): "such as Documents" — Documents is inside OneDrive (refused) on many Windows 11 computers. /fix decision: Applied — "Choose a folder on this computer's own drive."
- **LOW-007** (`past_sessions.py` `_export`): the ledger recorded the folder as given while the location check judged its real path — a `subst` drive gone after a reboot would make the next start read the `.part` as gone and drop the row. /fix decision: Applied — `_export` resolves the folder (`os.path.realpath`) and exports into, and records, that.
- **LOW-008** (`docs/design-system.md`): missing lines the code shows — Delete recording's help, the unreadable label's "Recording kept for development.", `NO_KEPT_RECORDING`, the no-layer line verbatim, `DEVELOPMENT_SAVE_FAILED` — and the disarm list (also Export's click, a refresh, leaving the tab). /fix decision: Applied, together with LOW-005/006/011's new lines.
- **LOW-009** (`ui/past_sessions_view.py`): round 20's `export_confirm_question` sat inside a block of constants with no blank lines after it. /fix decision: Applied — `EXPORT_CHOOSE_LOCAL` moved above it.
- **LOW-010** (`tests/test_past_sessions.py`): the close-failure test's docstring described a flush failure the proxy does not raise. /fix decision: Applied — the docstring says what runs (the close fails, and the clean-up's close too).
- **LOW-011** (`past_sessions.py` `recover_exports`, `PAST_SESSION_REASONS["export_ledger_busy"]`): round 20's busy branch was silent at start-up, and a ledger that can NEVER be opened (a deny ACL, a folder in its place) would promise "just now" for ever. /fix decision: Applied — `ExportRecovery.busy` and its start-up line ("…it will check again next time."); the refusal reads "…try again in a moment; if this keeps happening, restart the computer"; `test_a_busy_ledger_has_its_own_start_up_line`.
